(4) Our Values (4) 我們的核心價值
Guiding us in everything we do, our values are set out below: 引領我們一言一行的核心價值載列如下：
Trust We work diligently to uphold our reputation for helping organisations maximise
their digital efficiency and successfully get more value from technology.
可靠務實 我們的聲譽奠基於我們一直努力不懈地協助企業充份發揮數碼化效益，並成功在科
技上取得最大回報。
Transparency We are open and direct in the way we communicate and conduct our business.
We encourage information sharing across the Company. We own up to our
mistakes, work to correct them and learn from them.
公開透明 我們以公開透明的方式溝通和經營業務。我們鼓勵公司內部資料共享。我們勇於承
擔，不斷致力改善，並從錯誤中學習。
Teamwork We are committed to close teamwork among ourselves, with our business
partners and customers, with regulators and others we work with.
團隊精神 我們致力於各部門、並與商業合作夥伴、客戶，以至監管機構及其他工作夥伴，建
立緊密的合作關係。
Thoughtfulness We give our team members the resources and support to understand the pain
points of our customers and offer practical solutions to overcome them. We
cultivate empathy and care for each other, our customers and all stakeholders
whose lives we touch.
誠懇關懷 我們為同事提供資源和支援，以了解客戶的問題所在，從而提供實用的解決方案。
我們培養同理心，互相關懷，並照顧客戶和持分者所需。
Technology Our role is to maximise digital efficiency with a science-based approach that
involves measuring performance and constantly seeking improvement. We
will continue to explore innovative new technology that can help raise digital
efficiency in Hong Kong.
科技為本 我們以科技為本的方法，量化成效，不斷尋求進步空間，以充分發揮數碼化效益。
我們將繼續研究創新科技，幫助香港提高數碼化效益。
Timely We respect people’s time. From developing effective e-solutions to responding
swiftly to customer requests, we seek to make best use of time. Working
with a sense of timeliness helps us meet deadlines and keeps us ahead of
technological change.
與時並進 我們珍惜時間。從發展有效電子方案，至迅速回應客戶需求，我們務求在有限的時
間內達成最大的效益。有效管理時間有助我們按時完成工作，並在日新月異的科技
中保持優勢。

and work profile. The Board members should have a 公司的需求及業務。根據多元化政策，除

to a maximum tenure of nine years, unless the Board 元化政策的實施及成效。於年內，提名委

Nomination Policy 提名政策

Governance (Continued)
Materiality Assessment
To evaluate the materiality of ESG issues that are significant to our
investors and stakeholders, the Company considers the reporting
principles outlined in the ESG Guide, the nature and core activities
of our business and the outcomes of stakeholders’ engagement.
Based on this materiality assessment, we will periodically review
and adjust our ESG strategy as needed to ensure it remains aligned
with stakeholders’ priorities and evolving business needs.
Identification: Based on the market trends, standards, media
coverage, benchmarking analysis and insights, fourteen ESG-related
issues were identified.
Prioritisation: The topics to be reported were determined and
prioritised from two dimensions, namely materiality to the Group’s
business and materiality to the stakeholders.
Validation: Regarding the stakeholders’ feedback and analysis, five
issues were identified as “high materiality”. The result was verified
by the Board.

管治（續）
重要性評估
為評估對投資者及持份者而言屬重要的環境、社會
及管治事宜的重要性，本公司會考慮環境、社會及
管治指引所概述的匯報原則、我們業務的性質及主
要活動，以及持份者參與的結果。我們根據此重要
性評估定期檢討並按需要調整我們的環境、社會及
管治策略，以確保其與持份者優先考量的事宜及不
斷發展的業務需求保持一致。
識別：根據市場趨勢、標準、媒體報道、基準分析
及了解，識別出十四項環境、社會及管治相關事宜。
優先排序：從對本集團業務的重要性及對持份者的
重要性兩個維度確定須報告的議題，並對該等議題
進行優先排序。
確認：有關在持份者的反饋及分析方面，識別出五
項「高度重要性」事宜。該結果經董事會確認。

in Hong Kong, including but not limited to the Waste Disposal 不限於香港法例第354章《廢物處置條例》、香港法

granted under the Share Option Scheme 2014. The Board decided 股權將根據二零一四年購股權計劃獲授出。董事會
at the Board meeting held on 22 March 2024 that no new share 在於二零二四年三月二十二日舉行的董事會會議上
option scheme would be adopted, however, the outstanding options 決定不採納任何新購股權計劃，然而，根據二零一

valid and exercisable up to the end of the relevant exercise periods. 效及可行使，直至有關行使期完結為止。

The purpose of the Share Option Scheme 2014 is: 二零一四年購股權計劃之目的如下：
(a) to assist the Company to attract and retain the best available (a) 協助本公司吸引及留聘最佳員工；及
personnel; and
(b) to provide additional incentives to employees, directors, (b) 為僱員、董事、專業顧問、業務夥伴及諮詢顧
consultants, business associates and advisers to promote the 問提供額外獎勵，以促進本集團成功，
success of the Group,
by providing them with an opportunity to have a personal stake 此計劃透過授出購股權要約為上述人士提供以個人
in the Company through an offer of grant of options. The shares 身份持有本公司權益的機會。根據二零一四年購股
issued under the Share Option Scheme 2014 are identical in nature 權計劃發行的股份在本質上與本公司其他股份相同。
with the other shares of the Company.
The total number of shares which may be issued in respect of all 可就根據二零一四年購股權計劃將予授出的所有
options to be granted under the Share Option Scheme 2014 shall 購股權而發行的股份總數，合共不得超過於二零一
not exceed in aggregate 10% of the issued capital of the Company 四年購股權計劃的採納日期（即二零一四年五月九
at its adoption date, being 79,207,319 shares on 9 May 2014 (the 日）本公司已發行股本的10%（即79,207,319股股份）
“Scheme Mandate Limit”). Options that have lapsed shall not be （「計劃授權限額」）。在計算計劃授權限額時，已失
counted for the purpose of calculating the Scheme Mandate Limit. 效的購股權將不會計算在內。
The subscription amount payable in respect of each share option 因行使購股權而須就每份購股權支付的認購款項須
upon exercise of an option shall be determined by the Board and 由董事會釐定，且不得少於下列較高者：
shall be not less than the greater of:

daily quotation sheets for the five business days immediately 所每日報價表所列的平均收市價。

asset. In calculating interest income, the effective 面總值（倘資產並無信貸減值）。然

a financial asset is credit-impaired. A financial asset 產有否出現信貸減值。當發生一項

have a detrimental impact on the estimated future 產生不利影響的事件時，財務資產
cash flows of the financial asset have occurred. 即被視為出現信貸減值。

(b) Goodwill (b) 商譽
The goodwill recognised by the Group arose from the 本集團確認的商譽乃因於二零零九年收購
acquisition of Digital Trade and Transportation Network 數碼貿易運輸網絡有限公司（「DTTNCo」）
Limited (“DTTNCo”) in 2009 and is attributable mainly 所產生並主要源自專業技術、知識產權及
to the technical expertise, intellectual property and the 預計合併DTTNCo至本集團現有業務及客
synergies expected to be achieved from integrating 戶基礎後可達致的協同效應。自收購日期
DTTNCo into the Group’s existing business and 二零零九年三月二十六日起，商譽的賬面
customer base. The goodwill has a carrying amount of 值為港幣9,976,000元。
HK$9,976,000 since the acquisition date of 26 March
2009.
The E-Commerce segment of the Group is expected to 由於預期本集團電子商貿分部可受惠於收
benefit from the synergies of the acquisition of DTTNCo 購DTTNCo全部股權所產生的協同效益，
in entirety, and there are no other units within the Group 加上未能將商譽適當分配至本集團其他單
that the goodwill can be appropriately allocated to. 位，故電子商貿分部獲識別為包含上述商
Accordingly, the E-Commerce segment is identified as the 譽的現金產生單位（「現金產生單位」），
cash-generating unit (“CGU”) containing the goodwill for 以便為上述商譽作出減值評估。
the purpose of impairment evaluation of the goodwill.
The recoverable amount of the CGU is determined based 現金產生單位的可收回數額乃根據使用價
on value-in-use calculations. These calculations use the 值計算法釐定，其按現金產生單位涵蓋六
CGU’s cash flow projections based on financial forecasts 年期間的財務預測的預測現金流計算。基
covering a six-year period. Based on the management’s 於管理層的最佳估計，六年期間（二零二
best estimates, cash flows beyond the six-year period 三年：六年期間）以後的現金流乃根據零
(2023: six-year period) are extrapolated at zero (2023: （二零二三年：零）增長率來推斷。未來現
zero) growth rate. The future cash flows are discounted, 金流量以評估期間本集團的特定貼現率
at a discount rate specific to the Group of 10% (2023: 10%（二零二三年：10%）貼現，以釐定現
10%) for the assessment period, to determine the value 金產生單位的價值。根據管理層的評估，
in use of the CGU. Based on management’s assessment, 於本年度毋須就商譽確認任何減值虧損

19 Provision for long service payments 19 長期服務金撥備
Hong Kong employees that have been employed continuously 根據香港《僱傭條例》，連續受僱至少五年的香
for at least five years are entitled to long service payments in 港僱員在若干情況下有權享有長服金。該等情
accordance with the Hong Kong Employment Ordinance under 況包括：僱員因嚴重不當行為或裁員以外的原
certain circumstances. These circumstances include where 因而被解僱；僱員於65歲或以上的年齡辭任；
an employee is dismissed for reasons other than serious 或僱傭合約有固定年期且屆滿後不予重續。長
misconduct or redundancy, that employee resigns at the age 服金應付款項的金額乃參考僱員的最終薪金
of 65 or above, or the employment contract is of fixed term （上限為港幣22,500元）及服務年限釐定，並扣
and expires without renewal. The amount of LSP payable 除集團向強積金計劃供款（見附註20）所產生
is determined with reference to the employee’s final salary 的任何應計福利金額，整體上限為每名僱員港
(capped at HK$22,500) and the years of service, reduced by 幣390,000元。目前，本集團並無任何獨立資
the amount of any accrued benefits derived from the Group’s 金安排以履行其長服金責任。
contributions to MPF scheme (see Note 20), with an overall
cap of HK$390,000 per employee. Currently, the Group does
not have any separate funding arrangement in place to meet
its LSP obligation.
In June 2022, the Government gazetted the Amendment 二零二二年六月，政府在憲報刊登修訂條例，
Ordinance, which will eventually abolish the statutory right 最終廢除僱主的法定權利，透過提取對強積金
of an employer to reduce its LSP payable to a Hong Kong 計劃的強制性供款，減少其應付香港僱員的長
employee by drawing on its mandatory contributions to the 服金。政府隨後宣佈修訂條例將自過渡日期起
MPF scheme. The Government has subsequently announced 生效。另外，預期政府亦將於廢除後推出補貼
that the Amendment Ordinance will come into effect from the 計劃以協助僱主。
Transition Date. Separately, the Government is also expected
to introduce a subsidy scheme to assist employers after the
abolition.
Among other things, once the abolition of the offsetting 其中，一旦取消抵銷機制生效，僱主自過渡日
mechanism takes effect, an employer can no longer use 期起不得再使用其強制性強積金計劃供款所
any of the accrued benefits derived from its mandatory 產生的任何應計利益（不論於過渡日期之前、
MPF contributions (irrespective of the contributions made 當日或之後作出的供款）以減少有關僱員服務
before, on or after the Transition Date) to reduce the LSP in 的長服金。然而，倘僱員於過渡日期前開始受
respect of an employee’s service from the Transition Date. 僱，僱主可繼續使用上述應計福利以扣減截至
However, where an employee’s employment commenced 過渡日期僱員服務的長服金；此外，過渡日期
before the Transition Date, the employer can continue to 前有關服務的長服金將根據緊接過渡日期前僱
use the above accrued benefits to reduce the LSP in respect 員的月薪及直至該日期的服務年期計算。
of the employee’s service up to that date; in addition, the
LSP in respect of the service before the Transition Date
will be calculated based on the employee’s monthly salary
immediately before the Transition Date and the years of
service up to that date.

20 Employee retirement benefits 20 僱員退休福利
The Group operates a Mandatory Provident Fund Scheme (the 本集團根據《香港強制性公積金計劃條例》，為
“MPF scheme”) under the Hong Kong Mandatory Provident 根據香港《僱傭條例》僱用的僱員，設立強制性
Fund Schemes Ordinance for employees employed under 公積金計劃（「強積金計劃」）。強積金計劃為獨
the jurisdiction of the Hong Kong Employment Ordinance. 立受託人管理的界定供款退休計劃。根據強積
The MPF scheme is a defined contribution retirement plan 金計劃，僱主與僱員須各自向計劃作出相等於
administered by independent trustees. Under the MPF scheme, 僱員有關入息5%的供款，而有關入息以每月港
the employer and its employees are each required to make 幣30,000元為上限。本集團向計劃作出的供款
contributions to the plan at 5% of the employees’ relevant 即時歸屬有關僱員。
income, subject to a cap of monthly relevant income of
HK$30,000. Contributions to the plan vest immediately.

Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and currency risk 在本集團的日常業務過程中，會遇上信貸、流
arises in the normal course of the Group’s business. These 動資金、利率及外匯風險。該等風險受到本集

and practices described below.
(a) Credit risk (a) 信貸風險
Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will 信貸風險指對手方將違反合約義務導致本
default on its contractual obligations resulting in a 集團蒙受財務虧損的風險。本集團的信貸
financial loss to the Group. The Group’s credit risk is 風險主要源自應收賬款及合約資產。由於
primarily attributable to trade receivables and contract 對手方為主要銀行，本集團面臨現金及現
assets. The Group’s exposure to credit risk arising from 金等值及銀行存款產生的信貸風險有限，
cash and cash equivalents and deposits with banks is 因此，本集團認為此乃代表信貸風險低。
limited because the counterparties are major banks, for
which the Group considers to represent low credit risk.
Trade receivables and contract assets 應收賬款及合約資產
Credit terms offered by the Group to customers are 本集團給予客戶的信貸期乃基於與客戶
based on individual commercial terms negotiated with 商訂的個別商業條款而定。信貸期通常為
customers. Credit periods generally range from one day 一天至一個月。
to one month.

the customer is automatically assigned with a credit limit 據客戶的按金或銀行擔保金額，自動為客
based on the amount of its deposit or bank guarantee. 戶分配一個信貸額度。本集團會按客戶使
The amount of deposit is determined on a customer-by- 用本公司服務的用量，而為客戶個別釐
customer basis, depending on its usage of the Group’s 定按金金額。一般而言，如客戶在正常付
services. Generally, if a customer reaches or exceeds 款週期前達到或超逾本身的信貸額度，
its credit limit before the normal billing cycle, an ad 將會向有關客戶發出臨時賬單，要求有關
hoc bill will be issued to the customers for payment by 客戶以銀行直接付款方式支付。然而，如
bank direct debit. However, if a customer is in default of 客戶因任何理由未有付款，則其賬戶將會
payment for whatever reason, its account is automatically 自動暫停運作，直至尚欠費用獲全數繳付
suspended from operation until all outstanding charges 為止。基於上述理由，客戶或會（亦往往
